Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the cohomologies structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 831. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 72 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 72. QED.
